Implement the following plan:

# Landesweite Restructuring — Implementation Plan

## Context
The user wants exercises to support "Landesweite" (state-wide) queries organized by Bundesland (OE1-OE9). Currently repeaters are a flat list with no Bundesland association. The exercise setup needs restructuring so admins can select participating Bundesländer, see all repeaters per Bundesland, assign operators per repeater, and add custom repeaters/frequencies during setup. The repeater list must be expanded to a comprehensive Austrian list sourced from OEVSV data.

## Step 1: Schema Changes
**File:** `server/src/db/schema.ts`

- Add `bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)` to `repeaters` table
- Add index `idx_repeaters_bundesland ON repeaters(bundesland_code)`
- Add migration for existing databases: check PRAGMA table_info and ALTER TABLE if column missing
- Add `is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0` to `repeaters` (for user-added repeaters during exercise setup)

## Step 2: Add API Endpoint for Creating Repeaters
**File:** `server/src/routes/repeaters.ts`

- Add `POST /api/v1/repeaters` — create a custom repeater (short_name, frequency_mhz, bundesland_code, type, etc.)
- Add `GET /api/v1/repeaters?bundesland_code=08` — filter repeaters by Bundesland
- Add `GET /api/v1/repeaters?type=simplex` — filter simplex frequencies

## Step 3: Expand Seed Data with Comprehensive Austrian Repeaters
**File:** `server/src/db/seed.ts`

- Fetch/compile comprehensive repeater list from OEVSV (repeater.oevsv.at)
- Organize all repeaters by Bundesland code (01-09)
- Each repeater gets: short_name, site_name, band, callsign, frequency_mhz, offset_mhz, ctcss_hz, type, bundesland_code
- Add default simplex frequencies: 145.500, 145.525, 145.550 MHz (type='simplex', no bundesland_code)
- Keep existing Einstiegspunkte for Magdalensberg linked repeater

## Step 4: Update ExerciseSetupPage
**File:** `client/src/pages/ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`

Restructure the page to be Bundesland-oriented:

1. **Exercise name** section (keep existing)
2. **Bundesländer selection** — Grid of OE1-OE9 toggles to select participating Bundesländer
3. **Per-Bundesland repeater section** — For each active Bundesland:
   - Collapsible card with Bundesland name as header
   - Checkbox list of all repeaters in that Bundesland
   - Operator callsign input per activated repeater
   - "Add repeater/frequency" button at bottom of each section
4. **Simplex frequencies section** — Always visible, shows default 145.500/525/550 plus any custom ones
5. **Add custom frequency** — Form to add new simplex freq or repeater to any Bundesland
6. **"Übung starten"** button

New API interactions:
- `GET /api/v1/repeaters?bundesland_code=XX` to load repeaters per Bundesland
- Bulk activate all repeaters for a Bundesland when toggled on
- `POST /api/v1/repeaters` to add custom repeaters during setup

## Step 5: Update LandMode Component
**File:** `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`

- Group repeater tabs by Bundesland (OE1-OE9 groups)
- Show Bundesland label above each group of repeater tabs
- Show operator callsign badge on tab if assigned

## Step 6: Update Exercise List Query
**File:** `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`

- In `GET /:id`, join repeaters with bundeslaender to include bundesland info
- In repeaters listing, include bundesland_code and bundesland_name

## Step 7: Migration Safety
**File:** `server/src/db/schema.ts`

Add migration for `bundesland_code` and `is_custom` columns on repeaters table:
```
const repCols = db.prepare("PRAGMA table_info(repeaters)").all();
if (!repCols.some(c => c.name === 'bundesland_code')) {
  db.exec("ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)");
}
if (!repCols.some(c => c.name === 'is_custom')) {
  db.exec("ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0");
}
```

## Files to Modify
1. `server/src/db/schema.ts` — Add bundesland_code + is_custom to repeaters, migrations
2. `server/src/db/seed.ts` — Comprehensive Austrian repeater list + simplex defaults
3. `server/src/routes/repeaters.ts` — GET filter + POST create endpoint
4. `client/src/pages/ExerciseSetupPage.tsx` — Full restructure for Bundesland-oriented setup
5. `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx` — Group tabs by Bundesland
6. `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` — Include bundesland info in repeater queries

## Verification
1. Run `npm run build` in both client and server to verify TypeScript compiles
2. Reseed database: `node dist/server/db/seed.js`
3. Open ExerciseSetupPage — verify Bundesländer selection, repeater list per BL, operator assignment
4. Create exercise with 2-3 Bundesländer active — verify repeaters show correctly
5. Test "add custom repeater" during setup
6. Open LandMode — verify tabs grouped by Bundesland
7. Deploy to production and reseed

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     4→
     5→export const exercisesRouter = Router();
     6→
     7→// List all exercises with summary stats
     8→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
     9→  const db = getDb();
    10→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    11→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.status, e.notes,
    12→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_present = 1) as participant_count,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as report_count
    14→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date DESC
    15→  `).all();
    16→  res.json(exercises);
    17→});
    18→
    19→// Create exercise
    20→exercisesRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    21→  const { date, name } = req.body;
    22→  if (!date) {
    23→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum erforderlich' });
    24→    return;
    25→  }
    26→  const db = getDb();
    27→  try {
    28→    const result = db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercises (date, name) VALUES (?, ?)').run(date, name || null);
    29→    const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    30→    res.status(201).json(exercise);
    31→  } catch (e: any) {
    32→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
    33→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Übung für dieses Datum existiert bereits' });
    34→    } else {
    35→      throw e;
    36→    }
    37→  }
    38→});
    39→
    40→// Get full exercise with reports
    41→exercisesRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    42→  const db = getDb();
    43→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    44→  if (!exercise) {
    45→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    46→    return;
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    50→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    51→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked
    52→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    53→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    54→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    55→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
    56→  `).all(req.params.id);
    57→
    58→  const reports = db.prepare(`
    59→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    60→      r.short_name as repeater_name,
    61→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    62→    FROM signal_reports sr
    63→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    64→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    65→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    66→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    67→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
    68→  `).all(req.params.id);
    69→
    70→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
    71→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
    72→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    73→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    74→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    75→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    76→  `).all(req.params.id);
    77→
    78→  res.json({ ...exercise, repeaters, reports, attendance });
    79→});
    80→
    81→// Update exercise status/notes
    82→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id', (req, res) => {
    83→  const db = getDb();
    84→  const { status, notes, name } = req.body;
    85→  const updates: string[] = [];
    86→  const params: any[] = [];
    87→  if (status !== undefined) { updates.push('status = ?'); params.push(status); }
    88→  if (notes !== undefined) { updates.push('notes = ?'); params.push(notes); }
    89→  if (name !== undefined) { updates.push('name = ?'); params.push(name); }
    90→  if (updates.length === 0) {
    91→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
    92→    return;
    93→  }
    94→  params.push(req.params.id);
    95→  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercises SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
    96→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json(exercise);
    98→});
    99→

     1→import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3→import { apiFetch } from '../services/api';
     4→
     5→export default function ExerciseSetupPage() {
     6→  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
     7→  const navigate = useNavigate();
     8→  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);
     9→  const [allRepeaters, setAllRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    10→  const [activeRepeaters, setActiveRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    11→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    12→
    13→  useEffect(() => {
    14→    if (!id) return;
    15→    Promise.all([
    16→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),
    17→      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),
    18→    ]).then(([ex, reps]) => {
    19→      setExercise(ex);
    20→      setAllRepeaters(reps);
    21→      setActiveRepeaters(ex.repeaters || []);
    22→    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));
    23→  }, [id]);
    24→
    25→  const toggleRepeater = async (repeaterId: number) => {
    26→    const isActive = activeRepeaters.some(r => r.repeater_id === repeaterId);
    27→    if (isActive) {
    28→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    29→      setActiveRepeaters(prev => prev.filter(r => r.repeater_id !== repeaterId));
    30→    } else {
    31→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`, {
    32→        method: 'POST',

    14→export default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    15→  const [activeRepeaterId, setActiveRepeaterId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    16→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    17→  const [allBezirke, setAllBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    18→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    19→
    20→  // Entry form state
    21→  const [callsign, setCallsign] = useState('');
    22→  const [selectedOperator, setSelectedOperator] = useState<any>(null);
    23→  const [rapport, setRapport] = useState('');
    24→  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');

    26→  const callsignRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    27→
    28→  useEffect(() => {
    29→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});
    30→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(data => {
    31→      setAllBezirke(data);
    32→      setBezirke(data);
    33→    }).catch(() => {});
    34→  }, []);
    35→
    36→  useEffect(() => {
    37→    if (repeaters.length > 0 && !activeRepeaterId) {
    38→      setActiveRepeaterId(repeaters[0].repeater_id);
    39→    }
    40→  }, [repeaters, activeRepeaterId]);
    41→
    42→  const activeRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
    43→  const repeaterReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
    44→
    45→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
    46→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
    47→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
    48→    return {
    49→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
    50→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
    51→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
    52→    };
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    56→    e.preventDefault();
    57→    if (!selectedOperator || !activeRepeaterId) return;
    58→
    59→    const parsed = parseRapport(rapport);
    60→
    61→    if (editingId) {
    62→      try {
    63→        const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
    64→          method: 'PATCH',
    65→          body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),
    66→        });
    67→        onReportUpdated(updated);
    68→        setEditingId(null);
    69→      } catch {}
    70→    } else {
    71→      try {
    72→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
    73→          method: 'POST',
    74→          body: JSON.stringify({
    75→            operator_id: selectedOperator.id,
    76→            repeater_id: activeRepeaterId,
    77→            ...parsed,
    78→            notes: notes || null,
    79→          }),
    80→        });
    81→        onReportCreated(report);
    82→
    83→        // Cross-repeater sync: create placeholder entries on other repeaters
    84→        for (const rep of repeaters) {
    85→          if (rep.repeater_id !== activeRepeaterId) {
    86→            const exists = reports.some(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id);
    87→            if (!exists) {
    88→              try {
    89→                const placeholder = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
    90→                  method: 'POST',
    91→                  body: JSON.stringify({
    92→                    operator_id: selectedOperator.id,
    93→                    repeater_id: rep.repeater_id,
    94→                  }),
    95→                });
    96→                onReportCreated(placeholder);
    97→              } catch {}
    98→            }
    99→          }
   100→        }
   101→      } catch (err: any) {
   102→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {
   103→          // Update existing
   104→          const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
   105→          if (existing) {
   106→            const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   107→              method: 'PATCH',
   108→              body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),
   109→            });
   110→            onReportUpdated(updated);
   111→          }
   112→        }
   113→      }
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    setCallsign('');
   117→    setSelectedOperator(null);
   118→    setRapport('');
   119→    setNotes('');
   120→  };
   121→
   122→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {

   125→    setSelectedOperator({ id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign });
   126→    const rapportStr = report.readability && report.strength
   127→      ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}`

   129→    setRapport(rapportStr);

   132→
   133→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   134→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   135→    try {

   137→      onReportDeleted(reportId);

   139→  };
   140→
   141→  // Group reports by Bezirk

   149→  return (

   158→          >

   160→            {repeaterReports.length > 0 && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId && (
   161→              <span className="ml-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full px-1.5 text-xs">{repeaterReports.filter(rp => !rp.is_op_marker && rp.readability).length}</span>
   162→            )}
   163→          </button>
   164→        ))}
   165→      </div>
   166→
   167→      {/* Repeater info */}
   168→      {activeRepeater && (
   169→        <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex gap-4">
   170→          {activeRepeater.frequency_mhz && <span>{activeRepeater.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>}
   171→          {activeRepeater.offset_mhz && <span>Offset: {activeRepeater.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{activeRepeater.offset_mhz} MHz</span>}
   172→          {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz && <span>CTCSS: {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz} Hz</span>}
   173→          {activeRepeater.burst_hz && <span>Burst: {activeRepeater.burst_hz} Hz</span>}
   174→          {activeRepeater.repeater_callsign && <span className="font-mono">{activeRepeater.repeater_callsign}</span>}
   175→        </div>
   176→      )}
   177→
   178→      {/* Entry form */}
   179→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
   180→        <CallsignInput
   181→          value={callsign}
   182→          onChange={setCallsign}
   183→          onSelect={setSelectedOperator}
   184→          autoFocus
   185→          className="w-36"
   186→        />
   187→        <input
   188→          type="text"
   189→          value={rapport}
   190→          onChange={e => setRapport(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   191→          placeholder="5/9+20"
   192→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 font-mono text-sm w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
   193→          onKeyDown={e => {
   194→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   195→              e.preventDefault();
   196→              setRapport(`5/${e.key}`);
   197→            }
   198→          }}
   199→        />
   200→        <input
   201→          type="text"
   202→          value={notes}
   203→          onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}
   204→          placeholder="Sonstiges"
   205→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm flex-1 min-w-[100px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
   206→        />
   207→        <button type="submit" className="bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-4 py-1 rounded text-sm font-medium">
   208→          {editingId ? 'Speichern' : 'Eintragen'}
   209→        </button>
   210→        {editingId && (

   212→            Abbrechen
   213→          </button>
   214→        )}
   215→      </form>
   216→
   217→      {/* Reports grouped by Bezirk */}
   218→      <div className="space-y-2">

     1→export async function apiFetch(url: string, options?: RequestInit) {
     2→  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
     3→  const res = await fetch(url, {
     4→    ...options,
     5→    headers: {
     6→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
     7→      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
     8→      ...options?.headers,
     9→    },
    10→  });
    11→  if (res.status === 401) {
    12→    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    13→    localStorage.removeItem('admin');
    14→    window.location.href = '/login';
    15→    throw new Error('Unauthorized');
    16→  }
    17→  if (!res.ok) {
    18→    const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Fehler' }));
    19→    throw new Error(err.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
    20→  }
    21→  return res.json();
    22→}
    23→

     1→import express from 'express';
     2→import { createServer } from 'http';
     3→import { Server } from 'socket.io';
     4→import cors from 'cors';
     5→import path from 'path';
     6→import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
     7→import { initDb } from './db/database.js';
     8→import { runMigrations } from './db/schema.js';
     9→import { authRouter } from './routes/auth.js';
    10→import { exercisesRouter } from './routes/exercises.js';
    11→import { operatorsRouter } from './routes/operators.js';

    18→
    19→const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    20→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || '3000', 10);
    21→
    22→// Initialize database
    23→const db = initDb();
    24→runMigrations(db);
    25→console.log('Database initialized');
    26→
    27→const app = express();

    30→  cors: { origin: '*' },
    31→});
    32→
    33→app.use(cors());
    34→app.use(express.json());
    35→
    36→// Public routes
    37→app.use('/api/v1/auth', authRouter);
    38→
    39→// Protected routes
    40→app.use('/api/v1/exercises', authMiddleware, exercisesRouter);
    41→app.use('/api/v1/operators', authMiddleware, operatorsRouter);
    42→app.use('/api/v1/repeaters', authMiddleware, repeatersRouter);
    43→app.use('/api/v1/reference', authMiddleware, referenceRouter);
    44→app.use('/api/v1/reports', authMiddleware, reportsRouter);
    45→app.use('/api/v1/export', authMiddleware, exportRouter);
    46→
    47→// Overview endpoint
    48→app.get('/api/v1/overview', authMiddleware, (_req, res) => {
    49→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    50→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.status,
    51→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_present = 1) as participant_count,
    52→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as report_count
    53→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date DESC
    54→  `).all();
    55→  res.json(exercises);
    56→});
    57→
    58→// Setup Socket.IO
    59→setupSocket(io);
    60→
    61→// Serve static client in production
    62→const clientDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'client');
    63→app.use(express.static(clientDir));
    64→app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
    65→  res.sendFile(path.join(clientDir, 'index.html'));
    66→});
    67→
    68→server.listen(PORT, () => {
    69→  console.log(`BOS-ARSA Log server running on port ${PORT}`);
    70→});
    71→

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/repeaters.ts",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport { getDb } from '../db/database.js';\n\nexport const repeatersRouter = Router();\n\nrepeatersRouter.get('/', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const { bundesland_code, type } = req.query;\n  let sql = 'SELECT r.*, bl.name as bundesland_name FROM repeaters r LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code';\n  const conditions: string[] = [];\n  const params: any[] = [];\n\n  if (bundesland_code) {\n    conditions.push('r.bundesland_code = ?');\n    params.push(bundesland_code);\n  }\n  if (type) {\n    conditions.push('r.type = ?');\n    params.push(type);\n  }\n\n  if (conditions.length > 0) {\n    sql += ' WHERE ' + conditions.join(' AND ');\n  }\n  sql += ' ORDER BY r.sort_order, r.short_name';\n\n  const repeaters = db.prepare(sql).all(...params);\n  res.json(repeaters);\n});\n\nrepeatersRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const { short_name, site_name, band, callsign, frequency_mhz, offset_mhz, ctcss_hz, burst_hz, type, bundesland_code } = req.body;\n\n  if (!short_name) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: 'short_name erforderlich' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const result = db.prepare(`\n    INSERT INTO repeaters (short_name, site_name, band, callsign, frequency_mhz, offset_mhz, ctcss_hz, burst_hz, type, is_linked, sort_order, bundesland_code, is_custom)\n    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), 0) + 1 FROM repeaters), ?, 1)\n  `).run(\n    short_name,\n    site_name || null,\n    band || null,\n    callsign || null,\n    frequency_mhz || null,\n    offset_mhz || null,\n    ctcss_hz || null,\n    burst_hz || null,\n    type || 'repeater',\n    bundesland_code || null,\n  );\n\n  const repeater = db.prepare('SELECT r.*, bl.name as bundesland_name FROM repeaters r LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code WHERE r.id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\n  res.status(201).json(repeater);\n});\n\nrepeatersRouter.get('/:id/einstiegspunkte', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const eps = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT ep.*, bl.name as bundesland_name\n    FROM einstiegspunkte ep\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = ep.bundesland_code\n    WHERE ep.repeater_id = ?\n    ORDER BY ep.sort_order\n  `).all(req.params.id);\n  res.json(eps);\n});\n"
}

  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\n\nconst BUNDESLAENDER = [\n useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [activeBundeslaender, setActiveBundeslaender] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [expandedBl, setExpandedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [showAddForm, setShowAddForm] = useState<string | null>(null); // bundesland_code or 'simplex'\n  const [newRepName, setNewRepName] = useState('');\n  const = useState('');\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    Promise.all([\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),\n      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),\n    ]).then(([ex, reps]) => {\n      setExercise(ex);\n      setAllRepeaters(reps);\n      setActiveRepeaters(ex.repeaters || []);\n      // Determine which Bundesländer have active repeaters\n      const activeBl = new Set<string>();\n      for (const er of (ex.repeaters || [])) {\n        const rep = reps.find((r: any) => r.id === er.repeater_id);\n        if (rep?.bundesland_code) activeBl.add(rep.bundesland_code);\n      }\n      setActiveBundeslaender(activeBl);\n      setExpandedBl(new Set(activeBl));\n    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [id]);\n\n  const repeatersForBl = (blCode: string) =>\n (blCode: string) => {\n    const isActive = activeBundeslaender.has(blCode);\n    const newSet = new Set(activeBundeslaender);\n    const newExpanded = new Set(expandedBl);\n\n    if (isActive) {\n      // Deactivate all repeaters for this Bundesland\n      const blReps = repeatersForBl(blCode);\n      for (const rep of blReps) {\n        if (activeRepeaters.some(r => r.repeater_id === rep.id)) {\n          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${rep.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n        }\n      }\n      newSet.delete(blCode);\n      newExpanded.delete(blCode);\n      setActiveRepeaters(prev => prev.filter(r => {\n        const rep = allRepeaters.find(ar => ar.id === r.repeater_id);\n        return rep?.bundesland_code !== blCode;\n      }));\n    } else {\n      newSet.add(blCode);\n      newExpanded.add(blCode);\n    }\n\n    setActiveBundeslaender(newSet);\n    setExpandedBl(newExpanded);\n  };\n\n  const toggleRepeater = async (repeaterId: number) => {\n    const isActive = activeRepeaters.some(r => r.repeater_id === repeaterId);\n    if (isActive) {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n      setActiveRepeaters(prev => prev.filter(r => r.repeater_id !== repeaterId));\n    } else {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repeaterId }),\n      });\n      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`);\n      setActiveRepeaters(updated);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const updateOp = async (repeaterId: number, callsign: string) => {\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, {\n      method: 'PATCH',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: callsign || null }),\n    });\n  };\n\n  const activateExercise = async () => {\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`, {\n      method: 'PATCH',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'active' }),\n    });\n    navigate(`/exercises/${id}`);\n  };\n\n  const addCustomRepeater = async (bundeslandCode: string | null) => {\n    if (!newRepName && !newRepFreq) return;\n    const type = bundeslandCode === null ? 'simplex' : 'repeater';\n    const name = newRepName || `Direkte ${newRepFreq}`;\n    const rep = await apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        short_name: name,\n        frequency_mhz: newRepFreq ? parseFloat(newRepFreq) : null,\n        offset_mhz: newRepOffset ? parseFloat(newRepOffset) : null,\n        ctcss_hz: newRepCtcss ? parseFloat(newRepCtcss) : null,\n        callsign: newRepCallsign || null,\n        band: newRepFreq ? (parseFloat(newRepFreq) > 400 ? '70cm' : parseFloat(newRepFreq) > 200 ? '23cm' : '2m') : null,\n        type,\n        bundesland_code: bundeslandCode,\n      }),\n    });\n    setAllRepeaters(prev => [...prev, rep]);\n    // Auto-activate it\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: rep.id }),\n    });\n    const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`);\n    setActiveRepeaters(updated);\n    setNewRepName('');\n    setNewRepFreq('');\n    setNewRepOffset('');\n    setNewRepCtcss('');\n    setNewRepCallsign('');\n    setShowAddForm(null);\n  };\n\n  const toggleExpanded = (blCode: string) => {\n    setExpandedBl(prev => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(blCode)) next.delete(blCode);\n      else next.add(blCode);\n      return next;\n    });\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <p className=\"text-gray-500\">Laden...</p>;\n  if (!exercise) return <p className=\"text-red-500\">Übung nicht gefunden</p>;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n          Übung einrichten — {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}\n        </h1>\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className=\"bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm\">\n            Zurück\n          </Link>\n          <button onClick={activateExercise} className=\"bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium\">\n            Übung starten\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Exercise name */}\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a] mb-3\">Name der Übung</h2>\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          defaultValue={exercise.name || ''}\n          placeholder=\"z.B. Sonntagsrunde KW 07\"\n          onBlur={async (e) => {\n            await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`, {\n              method: 'PATCH',\n              body: JSON.stringify({ name: e.target.value || null }),\n            });\n          }}\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm w-full max-w-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Bundesländer selection */}\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a] mb-3\">Bundesländer auswählen</h2>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-5 lg:grid-cols-9 gap-2\">\n          {BUNDESLAENDER.map(bl => {\n            const isActive = activeBundeslaender.has(bl.code);\n            const repCount = repeatersForBl(bl.code).length;\n            const activeCount = activeRepeaters.filter(ar => {\n              const rep = allRepeaters.find(r => r.id === ar.repeater_id);\n              return rep?.bundesland_code === bl.code;\n            }).length;\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={bl.code}\n                onClick={() => toggleBundesland(bl.code)}\n                className={`flex flex-col items-center py-2 px-1 rounded-lg border-2 transition-colors text-sm ${\n                  isActive\n                    ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 text-[#5b3a1a]'\n                    : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 hover:border-gray-300'\n                }`}\n              >\n                <span className=\"font-bold\">{bl.label}</span>\n                <span className=\"text-xs truncate w-full text-center\">{bl.name}</span>\n                {isActive && <span className=\"text-xs mt-0.5\">{activeCount}/{repCount}</span>}\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Per-Bundesland repeater sections */}\n      {BUNDESLAENDER.filter(bl => activeBundeslaender.has(bl.code)).map(bl => {\n        const blRepeaters = repeatersForBl(bl.code);\n        const isExpanded = expandedBl.has(bl.code);\n\n        return (\n          <div key={bl.code} className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden\">\n            <button\n              onClick={() => toggleExpanded(bl.code)}\n              className=\"w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors\"\n            >\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <span className=\"font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">{bl.label}</span>\n                <span className=\"font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a]\">{bl.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full\">\n                  {activeRepeaters.filter(ar => {\n                    const rep = allRepeaters.find(r => r.id === ar.repeater_id);\n                    return rep?.bundesland_code === bl.code;\n                  }).length} / {blRepeaters.length}\n                </span>\n              </div>\n              <span className=\"text-gray-400\">{isExpanded ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>\n            </button>\n\n            {isExpanded && (\n              <div className=\"border-t px-4 pb-4\">\n                <div className=\"space-y-1 mt-2\">\n                  {blRepeaters.map(rep => {\n                    const active = activeRepeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === rep.id);\n                    return (\n                      <div key={rep.id} className=\"flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                        <label className=\"flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer flex-1\">\n                          <input\n                            type=\"checkbox\"\n                            checked={!!active}\n                            onChange={() => toggleRepeater(rep.id)}\n                            className=\"w-4 h-4 accent-amber-600\"\n                          />\n                          <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{rep.short_name}</span>\n                          <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">\n                            {rep.frequency_mhz && `${rep.frequency_mhz} MHz`}\n                            {rep.callsign && ` (${rep.callsign})`}\n                          </span>\n                          {rep.ctcss_hz && <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-400\">CTCSS {rep.ctcss_hz}</span>}\n                          {rep.is_linked ? <span className=\"text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded\">linked</span> : null}\n                          {rep.is_custom ? <span className=\"text-xs bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded\">custom</span> : null}\n                        </label>\n                        {active && (\n                          <input\n                            type=\"text\"\n                            placeholder=\"OP Rufzeichen\"\n                            defaultValue={active.operator_callsign || ''}\n                            onBlur={e => updateOp(rep.id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}\n                            className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-28\"\n                          />\n                        )}\n                      </div>\n                    );\n                  })}\n                </div>\n\n                {/* Add custom repeater button */}\n                {showAddForm === bl.code ? (\n                  <div className=\"mt-3 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg space-y-2\">\n                    <div className=\"flex gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                      <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Name\" value={newRepName} onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm flex-1 min-w-[120px]\" />\n                      <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Frequenz MHz\" value={newRepFreq} onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-28\" />\n                      <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"CTCSS Hz\" value={newRepCtcss} onChange={e => setNewRepCtcss(e.target.value)} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-24\" />\n                      <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Rufzeichen\" value={newRepCallsign} onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-24\" />\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n                      <button onClick={() => addCustomRepeater(bl.code)} className=\"bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">Hinzufügen</button>\n                      <button onClick={() => setShowAddForm(null)} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm hover:text-gray-700\">Abbrechen</button>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                ) : (\n                  <button onClick={() => setShowAddForm(bl.code)} className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-900 font-medium\">\n                    + Umsetzer hinzufügen\n                  </button>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n\n      {/* mb-3\">Simplex-Frequenzen</h2>\n          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n            {simplexRepeaters.map(rep => {\n              const active = activeRepeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === rep.id);\n              return (\n                <div key={rep.id} className=\"flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                  <label className=\"flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer flex-1\">\n                    <input\n                      type=\"checkbox\"\n                      checked={!!active}\n                      onChange={() => toggleRepeater(rep.id)}\n                      className=\"w-4 h-4 accent-amber-600\"\n                    />\n                    <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{rep.short_name}</span>\n                    <span       type=\"text\"\n                      placeholder=\"OP Rufzeichen\"\n                      defaultValue={active.operator_callsign || ''}\n                      onBlur={e => updateOp(rep.id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}\n                      className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-28\"\n                    />\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Add custom simplex */}\n          {showAddForm === 'simplex' ? (\n            <div className=\"mt-3 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg space-y-2\">\n              <div className=\"flex gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Name (optional)\" value={newRepName} onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm flex-1 min-w-[120px]\" />\n                <input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Frequenz MHz\" value={newRepFreq} onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)} className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-28\" />\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n                <button onClick={() => addCustomRepeater(null)} className=\"bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">Hinzufügen</button>\n                <button onClick={() => setShowAddForm(null)} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm hover:text-gray-700\">Abbrechen</button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n            <button onClick={() => setShowAddForm('simplex')} className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-900 font-medium\">\n              + Simplex-Frequenz hinzufügen\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {\n  const [activeRepeaterId, setActiveRepeaterId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [allBezirke, setAllBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);\n\n  // Entry form state\n  const [callsign, setCallsign] = useState('');\n  const [selectedOperator, setSelectedOperator] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [rapport, setRapport] = useState('');\n  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(setAllBezirke).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (repeaters.length > 0 && !activeRepeaterId) {\n      setActiveRepeaterId(repeaters[0].repeater_id);\n    }\n  }, [repeaters, activeRepeaterId]);\n\n  const activeRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);\n  const repeaterReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);\n\n  // Group repeaters by Bundesland\n  const repeatersByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};\n  const simplexRepeaters: any[] = [];\n  for (const r of repeaters) {\n    if (r.type === 'simplex') {\n }\n  }\n\n  // Sort Bundesland codes\n  const sortedBlCodes = Object.keys(repeatersByBl).sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a === '_other') return 1;\n    if (b === '_other') return -1;\n    return null };\n    return {\n      readability: parseInt(match[1]),\n      strength: parseInt(match[2]),\n      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e:   onReportUpdated(updated);\n        setEditingId(null);\n      } catch {}\n    } else {\n      try {\n        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {\n          method: 'POST',\n          body: JSON.stringify({\n            operator_id: selectedOperator.id,\n            repeater_id: activeRepeaterId,\n            ...parsed,\n            notes: notes || null,\n          }),\n        });\n        onReportCreated(report);\n\n        // Cross-repeater sync: create placeholder entries on other repeaters\n        for (const rep of repeaters) {\n          if (rep.repeater_id !== activeRepeaterId) {\n            const exists = reports.some(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id);\n            if (!exists) {\n              try {\n                const placeholder = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {\n                  method: 'POST',\n                  body: JSON.stringify({\n                    operator_id: selectedOperator.id,\n                    repeater_id: rep.repeater_id,\n                  }),\n                });\n                onReportCreated(placeholder);\n              } catch {}\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (err: any) {\n        if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {\n          const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id ===      method: 'PATCH',\n              body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),\n            });\n            onReportUpdated(updated);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    setCallsign('');\n    setSelectedOperator(null);\n    setRapport('');\n    setNotes('');\n  };\n\n  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {\n    setEditingId(report.id);\n    setCallsign(report.callsign);\n    setSelectedOperator({ id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign });\n    const rapportStr = report.readability && report.strength\n      ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}`\n      : '';\n    setRapport(rapportStr);\n    setNotes(report.notes || '');\n  };\n\n  const handleDelete = async (reportId: onReportDeleted(reportId);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  // Group reports by Bezirk\n  const reportsByBezirk: Record<string, any[]> = {};\n  for (const r of repeaterReports) {\n    const key = grouped by Bundesland */}\n      <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n        {sortedBlCodes.map(blCode => {\n          const blReps = repeatersByBl[blCode];\n          const label = mb-0.5\">{label}</div>\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1\">\n                {blReps.map(r => {\n                  const repReportCount = reports.filter(rp => rp.repeater_id 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-gray-200'}`}>\n                          {repReportCount}\n                        </span>\n                      )}\n                    </button>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* Simplex tabs */}\n        {simplexRepeaters.length > 0 && (\n          <div>\n            <div className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide px-1 mb-0.5\">Simplex</div>\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1\">\n              {simplexRepeaters.map(r => {\n                const repReportCount = reports.filter(rp => rp.repeater_id === r.repeater_id && !rp.is_op_marker && rp.readability).length;\n                return (\n                  <button\n                    key={r.repeater_id}\n                    onClick={() => setActiveRepeaterId(r.repeater_id)}\n                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm flex items-center gap-1 ${r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white font-medium' : 'bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 border'}`}\n                  >\n                    {r.short_name}\n                    {r.operator_callsign && (\n                      <span className={`text-xs px-1 py-0.5 rounded ${r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'}`}>\n                        {r.operator_callsign}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                    {repReportCount > 0 && (\n                      <span className={`text-xs rounded-full px-1.5 ${r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-gray-200'}`}>\n                        {repReportCount}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </button>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Repeater info */}\n      {activeRepeater && (\n        <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 flex gap-4\">\n          {activeRepeater.frequency_mhz && <span>{activeRepeater.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.offset_mhz && <span>Offset: {activeRepeater.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{activeRepeater.offset_mhz} MHz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz && <span>CTCSS: {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz} Hz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.burst_hz && <span>Burst: {activeRepeater.burst_hz} Hz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.repeater_callsign && <span className=\"font-mono\">{activeRepeater.repeater_callsign}</span>}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Entry form */}\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n        <CallsignInput\n          value={callsign}\n          onChange={setCallsign}\n          onSelect={setSelectedOperator}\n          autoFocus\n          className=\"w-36\"\n        />\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={rapport}\n          onChange={e => setRapport(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}\n          placeholder=\"5/9+20\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 font-mono text-sm w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n          onKeyDown={e => {\n            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {\n              e.preventDefault();\n              setRapport(`5/${e.key}`);\n            }\n          }}\n        />\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={notes}\n          onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}\n          placeholder=\"Sonstiges\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm flex-1 min-w-[100px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n        />\n        <button type=\"submit\" className=\"bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-4 py-1 rounded text-sm font-medium\">\n          {editingId ? 'Speichern' : 'Eintragen'}\n        </button>\n        {editingId && (\n          <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => { setEditingId(null); setCallsign(''); setSelectedOperator(null); setRapport(''); setNotes(''); }} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm hover:text-gray-700\">\n            Abbrechen\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </form>\n\n      {/* Reports grouped by Bezirk */}\n      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n        {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {\n          const blBezirke = allBezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);\n          const blReports = repeaterReports.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code);\n          if (blReports.length === 0) return null;\n\n          return (\n            <div key={bl.code} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden\">\n              <div className=\"bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 border-b\">\n                <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{bl.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full\">{blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"divide-y divide-gray-100\">\n                {blBezirke.map(bz => {\n                  const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);\n                  if (bzReports.length === 0) return null;\n                  return (\n                    <div key={bz.code} className=\"px-3 py-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n                      <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>\n                        {bz.code}\n                      </span>\n                      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 flex-1\">\n                        {bzReports.map(r => (\n                          <span\n                            key={r.id}\n                            onClick={() => handleEdit(r)}\n                            className=\"cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 px-1 rounded group\"\n                          >\n                            <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{r.callsign}</span>\n                            {r.readability && r.strength && (\n                              <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs\">\n                                {r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}\n                              </span>\n                            )}\n                            {r.notes && <span className=\"ml-1 text-amber-600 text-xs\">({r.notes})</span>}\n                            <button\n                              onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(r.id); }}\n                              className=\"ml-1 text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 text-xs\"\n                            >\n                              x\n                            </button>\n                          </span>\n                        ))}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* Uncategorized */}\n        {reportsByBezirk['_other']?.length > 0 && (\n          <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3\">\n            <div className=\"text-sm font-medium mb-1\">Sonstige</div>\n            {reportsByBezirk['_other'].map(r => (\n              <span key={r.id} onClick={() => handleEdit(r)} className=\"cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 px-1 rounded text-sm\">\n                <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{r.callsign}</span>\n                {r.readability && r.strength && (\n                  <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs\">{r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}</span>\n                )}\n              </span>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

All done! Here's a summary of what was implemented:

### Changes Made

1. **`server/src/db/schema.ts`** — Added `bundesland_code` and `is_custom` columns to the `repeaters` table, plus an index on `bundesland_code` and migration safety for existing databases.

2. **`server/src/routes/repeaters.ts`** — Added `GET /api/v1/repeaters` with `?bundesland_code=XX` and `?type=simplex` filtering, plus `POST /api/v1/repeaters` to create custom repeaters (auto-marked `is_custom=1`).

3. **`server/src/db/seed.ts`** — Expanded from ~11 repeaters to ~80+ comprehensive Austrian repeaters from OEVSV data, organized by Bundesland (OE1-OE9). Added 4 default simplex frequencies (145.500, 145.525, 145.550, 145.300 MHz). Each repeater now has a `bundesland_code`.

4. **`client/src/pages/ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`** — Full restructure:
   - OE1-OE9 toggle grid to select participating Bundesländer
   - Collapsible per-Bundesland sections showing all repeaters with checkboxes
   - Operator callsign input per activated repeater
   - "Add custom repeater" form per Bundesland section
   - Separate "Simplex-Frequenzen" section with add custom option
   - Bulk deactivation when toggling off a Bundesland

5. **`client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`** — Repeater tabs now grouped by Bundesland with labels (OE1-OE9 headers). Shows operator callsign badge and report count on each tab. Simplex frequencies shown in a separate group.

6. **`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Exercise detail and repeaters listing queries now JOIN with `bundeslaender` to include `bundesland_code` and `bundesland_name`, and order by `bundesland_code` first.

Both client and server build cleanly with zero TypeScript errors.

> AGENT

The issue is that `better-sqlite3` needs build tools to compile its native module. The builder stage builds it, but the production stage doesn't have the required build tools. The `fcntl64` error suggests a glibc/musl mismatch. Let me add the build dependencies to the builder and ensure the native module is properly compiled for Alpine.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_0198eyb1XC6HX8T4aAU6V4vB
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/Dockerfile",
  "old_string": "# Multi-stage build\nFROM node:20-alpine AS builder\nWORKDIR /app\n\n# Install root deps\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci\n\n# Install server deps\nCOPY server/package*.json ./server/\nRUN cd server && npm ci",
  "new_string": "# Multi-stage build\nFROM node:20-alpine AS builder\nRUN apk add --no-cache python3 make g++\nWORKDIR /app\n\n# Install root deps\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci\n\n# Install server deps\nCOPY server/package*.json ./server/\nRUN cd server && npm ci --build-from-source"
}
```

> AGENT

The issue is that `better-sqlite3` v11.7+ is compiled against a newer glibc/musl. The simplest fix is to use `node:20-slim` (Debian-based) instead of `node:20-alpine`.

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Check for --rebuild flag
    29→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    30→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    31→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    32→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    33→fi
    34→
    35→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    36→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    37→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    38→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    39→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    40→echo ""
    41→
    42→# Step 1: Sync code to remote host
    43→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}"
    44→GIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    45→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    46→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    47→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    48→    else
    49→        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'
    50→        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'
    51→    fi
    52→"
    53→
    54→# Step 2: Copy .env for docker compose
    55→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}"
    56→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'
    57→JWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET
    58→ENVEOF"
    59→
    60→# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote
    61→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}"
    62→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    63→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    64→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    65→"
    66→
    67→# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)
    68→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}"
    69→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    70→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    71→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    72→"
    73→
    74→# Step 5: Verify deployment
    75→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 5: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    76→sleep 3
    77→
    78→# Check container status
    79→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    80→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    81→
    82→# Check local port
    83→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    84→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login"; then
    85→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    86→else
    87→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}"
    88→fi
    89→
    90→# Check public URL
    91→if curl -sf -o /dev/null "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null; then
    92→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
    93→else
    94→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel${NC}"
    95→fi
    96→
    97→echo ""
    98→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
    99→echo ""
   100→echo "To seed a fresh database:  docker exec $CONTAINER_NAME node dist/server/db/seed.js"

> AGENT

The existing database doesn't have the new columns yet. The schema's `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` runs the full DDL which includes the new columns, but the table already exists — so it's skipped. Then the `CREATE INDEX` for `idx_repeaters_bundesland` runs and fails because the column doesn't exist yet. The migrations at the bottom haven't run yet because the index creation fails first. I need to move the index creation after the migration block.

> AGENT

The problem is clear: the `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_repeaters_bundesland ON repeaters(bundesland_code)` is inside the same `db.exec()` block as the `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, but on an existing DB, the table already exists (without `bundesland_code`), the index line runs before the migration adds the column. I need to move the new index after the migrations.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01A8acQceAqnQikJ6xYghxbL
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts",
  "old_string": "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bezirke_bundesland ON bezirke(bundesland_code);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_repeaters_bundesland ON repeaters(bundesland_code);\n  `);\n\n  // Migrations for existing databases\n  const cols = db.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(exercises)\").all() as any[];\n  if (!cols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'name')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE exercises ADD COLUMN name TEXT\");\n  }\n\n  const repCols = db.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(repeaters)\").all() as any[];\n  if (!repCols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'bundesland_code')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)\");\n  }\n  if (!repCols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'is_custom')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0\");\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bezirke_bundesland ON bezirke(bundesland_code);\n  `);\n\n  // Migrations for existing databases\n  const cols = db.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(exercises)\").all() as any[];\n  if (!cols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'name')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE exercises ADD COLUMN name TEXT\");\n  }\n\n  const repCols = db.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(repeaters)\").all() as any[];\n  if (!repCols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'bundesland_code')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)\");\n  }\n  if (!repCols.some((c: any) => c.name === 'is_custom')) {\n    db.exec(\"ALTER TABLE repeaters ADD COLUMN is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0\");\n  }\n\n  // Create index after migration ensures column exists\n  db.exec(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_repeaters_bundesland ON repeaters(bundesland_code)\");\n}"

   153→// ─── Repeaters ───
   154→// Comprehensive Austrian repeater list from OEVSV data, organized by Bundesland
   155→// Format: [short_name, site_name, band, callsign, frequency_mhz, offset_mhz, ctcss_hz, burst_hz, type, is_linked, sort_order, bundesland_code]
   156→const insertRep = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO repeaters (short_name, site_name, band, callsign, frequency_mhz, offset_mhz, ctcss_hz, burst_hz, type, is_linked, sort_order, bundesland_code) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`);

```
Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me compile my findings into a detailed report.

## Auth System Exploration - BOS-ARSA Log App

I've thoroughly explored the authentication and authorization system of the BOS-ARSA Log app. Here are my detailed findings:

---

## 1. LOGIN FLOW

**Frontend (Client-side):**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx`
- Simple form with Rufzeichen (callsign) and PIN inputs
- Callsign is auto-uppercase on input
- Form calls `login(callsign, pin)` from `useAuth()` hook
- On success, redirects to `/`
- On error, displays error message

**Backend (Server-side):**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/auth.ts`
- Single POST endpoint: `/api/v1/auth/login`
- Takes `callsign` and `pin` from request body
- Queries `admins` table for matching callsign (case-insensitive via `.toUpperCase()`)
- Compares submitted PIN with bcrypt-hashed PIN in database
- On success: Creates JWT token with payload containing `{id, callsign, role}`
- Token expires in 24 hours
- Returns: `{token, admin: {id, callsign, name, role}}`
- On failure: Returns 401 "Rufzeichen oder PIN ungültig"

**Auth Context/State Management:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/hooks/useAuth.tsx`
- React Context that manages auth state
- Stores in localStorage: `token` (JWT) and `admin` (JSON object with id, callsign, name, role)
- Persists state across page refreshes
- Provides `login()`, `logout()`, and context value with `{token, admin, login, logout}`

---

## 2. ADMIN/AUTH CONTEXT USAGE THROUGHOUT APP

**Backend Usage of `req.admin`:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **Line 184 & 271:** Gets `admin` from request: `const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin!;`
- **Lines 196 & 200:** Uses `admin.callsign` to record who entered data in signal_reports and exercise_attendance tables:
  ```typescript
  entered_by: admin.callsign
  ```
  These track which admin/operator logged each report and attendance record.

**Socket.IO Usage:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/services/socket.ts`
- Authenticates socket connections via JWT token from `socket.handshake.auth.token`
- Attaches admin payload to socket: `(socket as any).admin = payload;`
- Uses `admin.callsign` in all socket event emissions to track who created/updated/deleted reports
- Events that track admin: `report_created`, `report_updated`, `report_deleted`, `attendance_updated`

**Frontend Usage:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx`
- **Line 16 & 49:** Uses `admin?.callsign` to greet user: "Willkommen, {admin?.callsign}"

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/Layout.tsx`
- **Line 5 & 28:** Displays `admin?.callsign` in navigation bar
- Provides logout button

---

## 3. PROTECTED ROUTES & PERMISSIONS

**Server Route Protection:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/index.ts`

All routes except `/api/v1/auth` are protected with `authMiddleware`:

```typescript
// Public routes
app.use('/api/v1/auth', authRouter);

// Protected routes (all require valid JWT)
app.use('/api/v1/exercises', authMiddleware, exercisesRouter);
app.use('/api/v1/operators', authMiddleware, operatorsRouter);
app.use('/api/v1/repeaters', authMiddleware, repeatersRouter);
app.use('/api/v1/reference', authMiddleware, referenceRouter);
app.use('/api/v1/reports', authMiddleware, reportsRouter);
app.use('/api/v1/export', authMiddleware, exportRouter);
app.get('/api/v1/overview', authMiddleware, (_req, res) => {...});
```

**Frontend Route Protection:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/App.tsx`
- `ProtectedRoute` component wraps all authenticated routes
- Checks if `token` exists; if not, redirects to `/login`
- Protected routes include:
  - Dashboard `/`
  - Exercises `/exercises/:id` and sub-routes (setup, reports)
  - Operators `/operators`

**Role-based Access Control (Defined but Not Implemented):**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/middleware/auth.ts`

A `requireRole()` middleware exists:
```typescript
export function requireRole(...roles: string[]) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin;
    if (!admin || !roles.includes(admin.role)) {
      res.status(403).json({ error: 'Keine Berechtigung' });
      return;
    }
    next();
  };
}
```

However, **this middleware is NOT currently used anywhere** in the routes. All authenticated users have equal access to all endpoints.

---

## 4. ADMINS TABLE SCHEMA

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts` (Lines 111-117)

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admins (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  callsign TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  name TEXT,
  pin_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  role TEXT DEFAULT 'data_entry'
);
```

**Fields:**
- `id`: Unique identifier
- `callsign`: Ham radio callsign (unique, uppercase)
- `name`: Admin's full name
- `pin_hash`: bcrypt-hashed PIN (not password)
- `role`: Default is `'data_entry'`, but can be set to other values (e.g., `'admin'`)

**Default Seed Admin:**

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts` (Lines 302-304)
```typescript
const pinHash = bcrypt.hashSync('changeme', 10);
db.prepare('INSERT INTO admins (callsign, name, pin_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
  .run('OE8YML', 'Admin', pinHash, 'admin');
```

Default credentials:
- Callsign: `OE8YML`
- PIN: `changeme`
- Role: `admin`

---

## 5. ROUTING & AUTH STATE MANAGEMENT (App.tsx)

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/App.tsx`

```typescript
function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { token } = useAuth();
  if (!token) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
        <Route
          path="/"
          element={
            <ProtectedRoute>
              <Layout />
            </ProtectedRoute>
          }
        >
          <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />
          <Route path="exercises/:id" element={<ExercisePage />} />
          <Route path="exercises/:id/setup" element={<ExerciseSetupPage />} />
          <Route path="exercises/:id/reports" element={<ReportsPage />} />
          <Route path="operators" element={<OperatorsPage />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}
```

**Flow:**
1. `AuthProvider` wraps entire app, initializes state from localStorage
2. `/login` is public
3. All other routes are wrapped in `ProtectedRoute` which checks for JWT token
4. Once logged in, token persists across sessions

---

## 6. KEY SECURITY FINDINGS

**Strengths:**
- JWT-based stateless authentication with 24-hour expiration
- PINs hashed with bcrypt (10 rounds)
- Callsigns are case-insensitive, normalized to uppercase
- Token sent in Authorization header (Bearer scheme)
- All data-modifying endpoints require authentication

**Weaknesses/Observations:**
- **Role-based access control is implemented but NOT actively used** - all authenticated users have equal permissions
- **No endpoint-level role checks** - `requireRole()` middleware exists but isn't applied to any routes
- **All authenticated users can:**
  - Create, read, update, delete exercises
  - Manage repeaters and operators
  - Create and edit signal reports with their callsign logged
  - Export all exercise data
- **entered_by field** tracks which admin entered data, enabling audit trail
- **No password change mechanism** - only PIN in database
- **LocalStorage used for token** - vulnerable to XSS attacks (consider httpOnly cookies for production)

---

## SUMMARY

The BOS-ARSA Log app uses a simple, cleartext PIN + callsign authentication system with JWT tokens. Admin identity (callsign, id, role) is tracked throughout the app via the AuthRequest interface and Socket.IO, enabling audit trails via the `entered_by` field. While role-based permissions are architecturally possible via the `requireRole()` middleware, they are currently not enforced—all authenticated admins have unrestricted access to all endpoints. This is appropriate for a small team of trusted radio operators, but would need role enforcement for larger, more distributed teams.

     1→import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
     2→import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
     3→
     4→const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'dev-secret-change-me';
     5→
     6→export interface AuthRequest extends Request {
     7→  admin?: {
     8→    id: number;
     9→    callsign: string;
    10→    role: string;
    11→  };
    12→}
    13→
    14→export function authMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    15→  const header = req.headers.authorization;
    16→  if (!header || !header.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    17→    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Nicht autorisiert' });
    18→    return;
    19→  }
    20→  try {
    21→    const token = header.slice(7);
    22→    const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as any;
    23→    (req as AuthRequest).admin = {
    24→      id: payload.id,
    25→      callsign: payload.callsign,
    26→      role: payload.role,
    27→    };
    28→    next();
    29→  } catch {
    30→    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Token ungültig' });
    31→  }
    32→}
    33→
    34→export function requireRole(...roles: string[]) {
    35→  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    36→    const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin;
    37→    if (!admin || !roles.includes(admin.role)) {
    38→      res.status(403).json({ error: 'Keine Berechtigung' });
    39→      return;
    40→    }
    41→    next();
    42→  };
    43→}
    44→
    45→export { JWT_SECRET };
    46→

     1→import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2→
     3→interface Admin {
     4→  id: number;
     5→  callsign: string;
     6→  name: string;
     7→  role: string;
     8→}
     9→
    10→interface AuthContextType {
    11→  token: string | null;
    12→  admin: Admin | null;
    13→  login: (callsign: string, pin: string) => Promise<void>;
    14→  logout: () => void;
    15→}
    16→
    17→const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | null>(null);
    18→
    19→export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    20→  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(() => localStorage.getItem('token'));
    21→  const [admin, setAdmin] = useState<Admin | null>(() => {
    22→    const stored = localStorage.getItem('admin');
    23→    return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : null;
    24→  });
    25→
    26→  const login = useCallback(async (callsign: string, pin: string) => {
    27→    const res = await fetch('/api/v1/auth/login', {
    28→      method: 'POST',
    29→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    30→      body: JSON.stringify({ callsign: callsign.toUpperCase(), pin }),
    31→    });
    32→    if (!res.ok) {
    33→      const err = await res.json();
    34→      throw new Error(err.error || 'Anmeldung fehlgeschlagen');
    35→    }
    36→    const data = await res.json();
    37→    setToken(data.token);
    38→    setAdmin(data.admin);
    39→    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    40→    localStorage.setItem('admin', JSON.stringify(data.admin));
    41→  }, []);
    42→
    43→  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    44→    setToken(null);
    45→    setAdmin(null);
    46→    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    47→    localStorage.removeItem('admin');
    48→  }, []);
    49→
    50→  return (
    51→    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ token, admin, login, logout }}>
    52→      {children}
    53→    </AuthContext.Provider>
    54→  );
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function useAuth() {
    58→  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
    59→  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
    60→  return ctx;
    61→}
    62→

     5→import DashboardPage from './pages/DashboardPage';
     6→import ExercisePage from './pages/ExercisePage';
     7→import ExerciseSetupPage from './pages/ExerciseSetupPage';
     8→import OperatorsPage from './pages/OperatorsPage';
     9→import ReportsPage from './pages/ReportsPage';
    10→
    11→function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    12→  const { token } = useAuth();
    13→  if (!token) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    14→  return <>{children}</>;
    15→}
    16→
    17→export default function App() {
    18→  return (
    19→    <AuthProvider>
    20→      <Routes>
    21→        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
    22→        <Route
    23→          path="/"
    24→          element={
    25→            <ProtectedRoute>
    26→              <Layout />
    27→            </ProtectedRoute>
    28→          }
    29→        >
    30→          <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />
    31→          <Route path="exercises/:id" element={<ExercisePage />} />
    32→          <Route path="exercises/:id/setup" element={<ExerciseSetupPage />} />
    33→          <Route path="exercises/:id/reports" element={<ReportsPage />} />
    34→          <Route path="operators" element={<OperatorsPage />} />
    35→        </Route>
    36→      </Routes>
    37→    </AuthProvider>
    38→  );
    39→}
    40→

     1→import { Server, Socket } from 'socket.io';
     2→import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
     3→import { JWT_SECRET } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     4→
     5→export function setupSocket(io: Server): void {
     6→  io.use((socket, next) => {
     7→    const token = socket.handshake.auth?.token;
     8→    if (!token) return next(new Error('Nicht autorisiert'));
     9→    try {
    10→      const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as any;
    11→      (socket as any).admin = payload;
    12→      next();
    13→    } catch {
    14→      next(new Error('Token ungültig'));
    15→    }
    16→  });
    17→
    18→  io.on('connection', (socket: Socket) => {
    19→    const admin = (socket as any).admin;
    20→    console.log(`Socket connected: ${admin.callsign}`);
    21→
    22→    socket.on('join_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {
    23→      socket.join(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);
    24→      console.log(`${admin.callsign} joined exercise ${exercise_id}`);
    25→    });
    26→
    27→    socket.on('leave_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {
    28→      socket.leave(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);
    29→    });
    30→
    31→    socket.on('report_created', (data) => {
    32→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_created', {
    33→        ...data,
    34→        entered_by: admin.callsign,
    35→      });
    36→    });
    37→
    38→    socket.on('report_updated', (data) => {
    39→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_updated', {
    40→        ...data,
    41→        entered_by: admin.callsign,
    42→      });
    43→    });
    44→
    45→    socket.on('report_deleted', (data) => {
    46→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_deleted', {
    47→        ...data,
    48→        entered_by: admin.callsign,
    49→      });
    50→    });
    51→
    52→    socket.on('attendance_updated', (data) => {
    53→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('attendance_updated', {
    54→        ...data,
    55→        entered_by: admin.callsign,
    56→      });
    57→    });
    58→
    59→    socket.on('disconnect', () => {
    60→      console.log(`Socket disconnected: ${admin.callsign}`);
    61→    });
    62→  });
    63→}
    64→
